## Failures and how to do differently

- symptom: incomplete inventory, offline test, proxy metric, or historical report is presented as a completed capability result -> fix: retain evidence tiers, run raw-token terminal-EOS and 4K-retention gates on actual generations, and state unfinished deliverables. [Task 1][Task 2][Task 4]
- symptom: broad exploration delays a demanded experiment -> fix: verify exact parent/checkpoint/data/runtime dependencies once, then launch or stop on concrete mismatch; do not improvise GPU debugging or install packages ad hoc. [Task 3]
# RTX 5090 Blackwell training profile

最后验证：2026-07-25，PyTorch `2.8.0+cu128`，CUDA capability `sm_120`。

这是本仓库后续 RTX 5090 训练的默认性能基线。第一性原则是用最快的稳定路径
最小化回答研究问题所需的总 GPU 时间与成本。它不固定科学 batch size；若已有
相同或足够相近 shape 的完成 receipt，直接复用。只有没有可复用证据时，才做
能够确认显存、吞吐、finite loss 和算子资格的最短丢弃式 probe。

torch.set_float32_matmul_precision("high")
torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = True
torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = True
torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_bf16_reduced_precision_reduction = True

torch.backends.cuda.enable_flash_sdp(True)
torch.backends.cuda.enable_mem_efficient_sdp(False)
torch.backends.cuda.enable_math_sdp(False)
torch.backends.cuda.enable_cudnn_sdp(False)

compiled_loss = torch.compile(
    loss_module,
    mode="default",
    dynamic=False,
    fullgraph=False,
)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(..., fused=True)

[exit 0] # BM 128K 退化诊断与交接

用户要求研究128K错误原因，随后要求尽快收尾、提交并推送，转家里PC继续。范围收缩为两条关键反例：密集多键误绑定与VT提前EOS；QA和FWE只做原始结果分析，不增加大矩阵。

## 已经直接观察到的事实

1. Qwen3B 128K总体BM70.83%，MrPro78.13%。仅改变中段频率，gain/输入/decoder相同。BM在零基槽24–39增加压缩，相对MrPro频率下降约1.5%–31.5%，最大在槽34；高/低两端保持。该数学事实不自动决定注意力贡献的符号。
2. 多键负例问bizarre-inhabitant→3954314，BM却取bizarre-slime→9289114，后者确为原文另一记录；不是格式/标点错误。
3. VT负例MrPro列全5变量，BM只列起点ZOFPD，6生成token即EOS，未耗尽30-token预算。另一VT行BM20%→80%，说明不能把所有追踪问题归成同一失败机制。
4. FWE负例中真实计数crenea10785、lgwizg4793、ftlltd2696，BM却选qaxgco880与euoavl1725；不是频次并列的小差异。QA负例用集合包含链回答P与co-NP是否相等，属于答非所问。

## 最小受控诊断

模型、BM/MrPro表和greedy预算固定。C/P都由同一原始token子序列构成：C改为连续紧凑位置；P保留原始position IDs。MK保留完整指令、正确记录、BM选错的那条相似记录和问题；VT保留完整few-shot示例、全部5条必要赋值和问题。没有删掉VT示例或链中间步骤。

BM再做O/L：O按原完整输入重放；L保留原密集预填充的选定KV，重新计算最后prompt query，只改变之后的可见key集合。原始query与生成token从O缓存剔除；自回归追加不更新既有前缀KV。L与P的内容/位置集合相同，但历史表示来源不同。

另做一个MK输入编辑：仅将选错记录的key替换成不相似的新key，值不变、token数和所有其他位置不变；问题和正确记录不变。两方法都跑，判断这个具体竞争key是否足以造成错误。所有布局和编辑在诊断输出前冻结。

先用真实compact输入验证手动greedy与HF generate、all-keep L逐token一致。若O不能复现原生成，则不能把L结果直接解释为修复原负例。C未恢复满分时不执行对应L；不能为取得通过而改变保留集合。

这些都是含答案信息的oracle诊断，不是新方法或总体恢复率。P成功/C成功只说明清除背景后可解；L成功说明本次晚期限制可见集合足够，不能直接推出attention是唯一瓶颈。L失败/P成功才支持本例所选晚期策略不足，并不否定所有晚期算法。位置差异和长距离干扰的相互作用仍可能存在。

## 执行资产

远端根目录`/root/autodl-tmp/bm_transfer_20260908/`，`diagnosis_inputs_02`为最终两案例输入，`diagnosis_run_02`为最终结果（01在GPU加载前被旧decoder guard拒绝，随后修正None表示关闭的兼容性）；`diagnosis_inputs_01`是在CPU布局阶段中止的旧五案例准备，不是科学负结果。CPU布局曾重复构建token索引导致慢，已改为一次索引和二分区间查询。

运行器`diagnose.py`保存输出、EOS、逐步top-5及EOS margin、原始重放是否完全匹配。参数位置、首token和EOS列表等10项相关CPU测试通过；GPU资格已通过，完整O重放与原生成逐token一致；最终解释如下。

## 已完成结果（145.48秒，14次诊断生成，另有3次资格对照）

| BM条件 | 多键误绑定 | VT提前结束 |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| O 原完整输入重放 | 0% | 20% |
| C 相同选定token、紧凑位置 | 100% | 100% |
| P 相同选定token、原始128K位置 | 100% | 100% |
| L 原密集预填充KV、读取时仅保留同一集合 | 0% | 60% |

MrPro在两例C/P也均100%。O的两次输出与原始冒烟生成逐token完全一致；HF generate、手动greedy、all-keep L的真实compact资格测试亦逐token一致。

**可支持的机制结论：** 在这两例中，原始远距离位置配合精简背景仍可解，所以不能将失败直接归因于位置编号达到128K或模型完全不会解题。P与L保持同一可见token/位置集合，却得到不同结果，说明从完整长背景形成的保留KV状态参与了失败。仅在读出时限制可见集合不足以满分恢复；VT的20%→60%同时说明晚期竞争也有贡献。

这不证明“预填充状态不可修复”，不否定其他晚期读取算法，也没有识别哪一层、哪一头或哪一槽最重要。P同时改变各层背景参与和状态演化，不能把效果只归结为softmax分母或某个注意力质量代理。

**竞争key单点编辑：** 将原选错记录的bizarre-slime改成neutral-slime，只有token74100改变，长度130871、其他token及位置完全相同，正确记录/问题/数字均不变。MrPro仍正确3954314；BM从9289114换成另一个错误数字4068207，该数字属于原文strange-alien记录。因而最初那一个相似key不是整个失败的唯一原因；移除它会改变错选对象，却没有恢复精确绑定。语义/位置/全局背景的具体权重仍未被隔离。

**频率影响的量级：** 正确多键记录始于token35173，原query到该处lag95697，BM相对MrPro的最大相位差约31.25rad（槽28）；VT起始赋值token3986，lag127052，最大约41.49rad。频率百分比看似有限，累计到这些距离的相位变化并不小；但P仍能满分，说明相位差大本身不是失败的充分条件。不能将最大相位差的槽28自动称最敏感槽。

# MrRoPE 论文3B上的BM冒烟结果

**结论：没有复现OLMo的长端收益，不建议据此直接进入全量。** Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上，BM改善32K，但128K低于MrPro 7.29个百分点。本轮仅判断固定BM在该小面板的迁移，不宣称所有BM变体无效，也不能单独归因于模型容量。

[exit 1] """Scalar or slotwise rotary amplitudes for explicitly declared experiments."""
import numpy as np
import torch

from scripts.experiments.cross_audit.tables import install_static, verify_static, tensor_sha


def gain_vector(table, device):
    values = np.asarray(table['gain_by_slot'], dtype=np.float32)
    if values.shape != (len(table['values_float32']),) or not np.isfinite(values).all() or np.any(values <= 0):
        raise ValueError('invalid slotwise rotary amplitudes')
    # OLMo uses rotate_half: each rotary pair occupies coordinates j and j+K.
    return torch.from_numpy(np.concatenate((values, values))).to(device)


def install(model, table):
    values = np.asarray(table['values_float32'], dtype=np.float32)
    rotary = install_static(model, values, table['gain'])
    if 'gain_by_slot' in table:
        rotary.attention_scaling = gain_vector(table, rotary.inv_freq.device)


def verify(model, table):
    values = np.asarray(table['values_float32'], dtype=np.float32)
    if 'gain_by_slot' not in table:
        return verify_static(model, values, table['gain'])
    rotary = model.model.rotary_emb
    if rotary.inv_freq.dtype != torch.float32 or tensor_sha(rotary.inv_freq.detach().cpu().numpy()) != table['tensor_sha256']:
        raise RuntimeError('runtime frequency drift')
    expected = gain_vector(table, rotary.inv_freq.device)
    if not torch.equal(rotary.attention_scaling, expected):
        raise RuntimeError('runtime slotwise amplitude drift')
"""Parameter-free discrete boundary-matched allocation on a native RoPE grid.

Verified local benefit: OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct, static S=4, six-task RULER
subsets at up to 16K. This is not a guarantee for other models or scales.

from __future__ import annotations

import torch


def boundary_matched_inv_freq(
    native_inv_freq: torch.Tensor, *, base: float, reference_length: int, scale: float
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, float, dict]:
    """Return an FP32 table, scalar cos/sin gain, and exact construction metadata.

    Supply the unscaled native FP32 frequencies. Install the returned table
    after converting the model to BF16/FP16, so phase frequencies stay FP32.
    This operator does not choose a deployment scale or alter KV caches.

    if (native_inv_freq.ndim != 1 or native_inv_freq.dtype != torch.float32
        or native_inv_freq.numel() < 2):
        raise ValueError('native frequencies must be a one-dimensional FP32 tensor')
    if not math.isfinite(base) or base <= 1 or reference_length <= 0 or not math.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1:
        raise ValueError('invalid native geometry or extension scale')
    native = native_inv_freq.detach().cpu()
    dim = 2*native.numel()
    expected = 1/(base**(torch.arange(0,dim,2,dtype=torch.float32)/dim))
    if not torch.allclose(native,expected,rtol=3e-7,atol=0):
        raise ValueError('input is not the declared native endpoint grid')
    if scale == 1:
        return native_inv_freq.clone(),1.,dict(method='boundary_matched',scale=1.,identity=True)
    turns = [float(w)*reference_length/(2*math.pi) for w in native]
    fast = [j for j,t in enumerate(turns) if t>32]
    slow = [j for j,t in enumerate(turns) if t<1]
    if not fast or not slow or slow[0]<=fast[-1]:
        raise ValueError('native grid has no valid MrPro 32/1-turn transition')
    low,high = fast[-1],slow[0]
    n = high-low
    exponents = []
    values = []
    for j,w in enumerate(native.tolist()):
        q = min(n,max(0,j-low))
        m = q*(q+1)*(3*n+2-2*q)/(n*(n+1)*(n+2))
        exponents.append(m)

旧回执本次未找到，就把其状态改成“从未运行”。

## 2. 循环的根因是推理链反复失效，且失效结论没有退出

**不是缺少又一个频率函数。** 已有证据反复否定的是从某个局部量到实际能力的
跳接；后续却把该局部量改名、增加自由度，或把错误前提重新当起点。最具体的复发：

| 谱系 | 原记录中的跳接 | 本次裁决 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 早期shape/theta | sigmoid短/长PPL为37648/62474，因比值小而写“更稳定、PASSED” | 低崩溃比可以来自短端已经坏掉，不能作能力收益。原始表保留在legacy摘要 |
| Phase16的99 runs | 混合pilot/confirmation评分与种子数，宣称near-optimal law | 7月原始重分析已否定该排名。公式对独立邻点只赢3/9配置；本次给仍可检索的3月报告补撤回提示 |
| Phase18–23 | 一次候选失败便认定训练量/架构为根因，再找新tau公式 | 长度、训练量、数据与架构同时变化，不能作单因素归因。tau=1时最高频率仍约.72967 rad/token，“高频几乎没有”与实际数组不符 |
| QuALITY | 选项首字符、长输入被裁成8K；修后接近随机又归因为容量上限 | 早期评分/长度错误不能算方法负结果；修后低分仍不识别“容量是唯一瓶颈”。已补可见纠正 |
| YaRN身份 | 7月已区分官方线性ramp与repo smoothstep，9月又把sqrt增益Y2称忠实 | 官方cos/sin幅度为a=1+.1ln(s)，logits乘a²。Y2是变体；不能用它的零分证明官方方法上限 |
| Qwen数值混叠 | 8月已发现stride16高频混叠；后续log-p2又用2048整点格 | arithmetic旧表、log-p2、FullLag是不同身份；数值修复还曾降低128K能力，不能自动晋级 |
| 静态几何/响应 | Gram、曲率、重构残差、phase risk、覆盖、平滑性变好⇒生成变好 | 已有直接反例；18样本64维行为梯度也已失败，不重新包装成“功能需求恢复” |
| Native compact | 旧guard拒绝Native后换Z，仍回答Native对照的问题 | Native该臂未跑。Z短训20/32与完整输入23/32的不拒绝不证明等效，更不证明LoRA只修格式 |
| 适配与读取 | NLL、源依赖、target block rank提高⇒正确输出 | 7月rank约2000仍全错；Far-pass多种适配也有完整生成地板。已测过E/B、全词表竞争者、答案轨迹margin和write decomposition |
| oracle routing | 强制gold block进入候选集合≈完美路由，仍错⇒attention不是瓶颈 | 原代码仍保留其他块并用普通QK softmax。该控制的无效不能关闭所有注意力处理 |

这些裁决不需要再跑一夜。它们也不支持“所有理论都错、全部实验无效”：不同
协议的正结果和有效负结果必须继续保留，不能用一次修bug把真实负结果洗掉。

## 3. 本轮修复了哪些实质问题

**ALS虚假收敛已修复。** `previous=inf`使首轮比较成为`inf<=inf`。原测试只有
一个迭代值也可通过单调检查。现在要求前值有限，并补一个后续确实改善的回归例：
src=[1,.4,.1]、dst=[.95,.16,.045]、lag0..31均匀、rank3，旧首轮2.848193371；
修后43轮2.836061317，独立显式旋转重算一致。某些full-rank小例首轮已经固定，
所以这不是“所有旧表值都错了”，更不是模型能力被修复。

**LoRA容量的错误界已撤回。** rank-r全局更新的每头切片可以各有rank-r，受
行列维数限制，不能直接除以头数。一般LoRA还可以读取旧Q/K投影没有保留的hidden
方向，不必能写成旧q/k上的固定M/N。best-found残差只给该代理最小误差的上界，
不能变成不可修复下界；各向同性平均也不是最坏情形界。相应代码说明和仍传播
这些强断言的owner已加更正。

**旧有效表不再被通用安装器误拒。** 恢复的log-p2在槽1/18有频率交叉，通用
`install_signed`原来强制频率降序，无法表达这个已有方法。现在验证64个有限频率，
按冻结数组原槽位安装，由SHA保护身份；不排序、不改数组。Carrier构造本身的
家族约束保留。本轮未重新运行旧p2，也不宣称取消guard就是效果提升。

AGENTS只增加前瞻规则：回查本地负结果及后续纠正、按真实干预去重、纠正可检索
旧句、修复过时guard而不偷换比较、用非平凡案例检查数值求解。失误细节留在本文
和原owner，没有把AGENTS写成事故日志。

## 4. 从正确事实出发，能保留什么

- **简单分配仍有强依据。** MrRoPE已有简单零训练结果；本地FineWeb复算中，
  粗ramp与细derived表只差约.000511/.000560 NLL，而同端点geometric在16K
  比derived差4.455995。它支持粗分配结构有用，未证明精细曲线或复杂校准必需。
- **频率与槽位的结合是真变量。** frequency-only permutation能严重掉分，
  但这不证明每槽存在唯一语义标签，不证明频率必须单调，更不证明所有方法都
  必须针对checkpoint拟合。[Round and Round](https://arxiv.org/html/2410.06205)
  观察到位置/语义分工，也有同一head混合两者；其p-RoPE结果来自从零训练，
  不能直接外推为冷换表保证。
- **注意力与位置联合处理已有成功先例。**
  [DCA](https://arxiv.org/html/2402.17463)按块关系使用不同query位置，并统一

[exit 0] """Explicit static transforms, with native/derived identities kept separate."""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import math
import numpy as np
import torch
from scripts.lib.rope.official_yarn import official_yarn_on_inv_freq


def tensor_sha(values):
    return hashlib.sha256(np.ascontiguousarray(values, dtype='<f4').tobytes()).hexdigest()


def native_table(dim, base):
    return (1 / (base ** (torch.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / dim))).numpy()


def check_table(values, dim):
    a = np.asarray(values)
    if a.dtype != np.float32 or a.shape != (dim // 2,) or dim % 2:
        raise ValueError('table dtype/shape mismatch')
    if not np.isfinite(a).all() or not np.all(a > 0) or not np.all(a[:-1] > a[1:]):
        raise ValueError('table must be finite, positive and strictly descending')


def transform(source, *, dim, base, reference_length, scale, method):
    check_table(source, dim)
    if not math.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1 or base <= 1 or reference_length <= 0:
        raise ValueError('invalid transform geometry')
    if method == 'identity':
        return source.copy(), 1.0, {'method': 'identity'}
    if method == 'yarn':
        a, gain, meta = official_yarn_on_inv_freq(
            torch.from_numpy(source.copy()), head_dim=dim, base=base, scale=scale,
            original_max_position_embeddings=reference_length)
        return a.float().numpy(), gain, meta
    if method not in ('mrpro', 'mruni'):
        raise ValueError('unknown transform')
    reference = native_table(dim, base).astype(np.float64)
    turns = reference * reference_length / (2 * math.pi)
    fast, slow = np.flatnonzero(turns > 32), np.flatnonzero(turns < 1)
    if not len(fast) or not len(slow):
        raise ValueError('MrRoPE reference lacks 32/1-turn boundaries')
    lo, hi = int(fast[-1]), int(slow[0])
    if hi <= lo:
        raise ValueError('empty MrRoPE band')
    n = hi - lo
    t = np.clip(np.arange(dim // 2) - lo, 0, n)
    exponent = t / n if method == 'mruni' else t * (t + 1) / (n * (n + 1))
    result = (source.astype(np.float64) / scale ** exponent).astype(np.float32)
    native = np.array_equal(source, native_table(dim, base))
    return result, 1 + .1 * math.log(scale), {
        'method': method, 'reference': 'native_endpoint_grid', 'low': lo, 'high': hi,
        'label': 'MrRoPE paper-equation reproduction' if native else 'shared-reference MrRoPE-component control',
        'cumulative_exponents': exponent.tolist(), 'source': 'arxiv:2601.22181v1 eq13-16,26',
    }


def install_static(model, values, gain):
    """Install after model dtype/device conversion; preserve FP32 phase accuracy."""
    rotary = model.model.rotary_emb
    if getattr(rotary, 'rope_type', 'default') != 'default':
        raise ValueError('dynamic/scaled checkpoint unsupported; cannot silently stack transforms')
    dim = model.config.hidden_size // model.config.num_attention_heads
    check_table(values, dim)
    if not math.isfinite(gain) or gain <= 0:
        raise ValueError('invalid rotary amplitude')
    rotary.inv_freq = torch.from_numpy(values.copy()).to(device=next(model.parameters()).device)
    rotary.original_inv_freq = rotary.inv_freq.clone()
    rotary.attention_scaling = float(gain)
    return rotary


def verify_static(model, values, gain):
    r = model.model.rotary_emb
    if r.inv_freq.dtype != torch.float32 or tensor_sha(r.inv_freq.detach().cpu().numpy()) != tensor_sha(values):
        raise RuntimeError('runtime table drift')
    if float(r.attention_scaling) != float(gain):
        raise RuntimeError('runtime amplitude drift')

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor],
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
        past_key_values: Cache | None = None,
        **kwargs: Unpack[FlashAttentionKwargs],
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        input_shape = hidden_states.shape[:-1]
        hidden_shape = (*input_shape, -1, self.head_dim)

        query_states = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape).transpose(1, 2)
        key_states = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape).transpose(1, 2)
        value_states = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape).transpose(1, 2)

        cos, sin = position_embeddings
        query_states, key_states = apply_rotary_pos_emb(query_states, key_states, cos, sin)

        if past_key_values is not None:
            key_states, value_states = past_key_values.update(key_states, value_states, self.layer_idx)

        attention_interface: Callable = ALL_ATTENTION_FUNCTIONS.get_interface(
            self.config._attn_implementation, eager_attention_forward
        )

        attn_output, attn_weights = attention_interface(
            self,
            query_states,
            key_states,
            value_states,
            attention_mask,
            dropout=0.0 if not self.training else self.attention_dropout,
            scaling=self.scaling,
            sliding_window=self.sliding_window,  # main diff with Llama
            **kwargs,
        )

        attn_output = attn_output.reshape(*input_shape, -1).contiguous()
        attn_output = self.o_proj(attn_output)
        return attn_output, attn_weights

    @torch.no_grad()
    @dynamic_rope_update  # power user: used with advanced RoPE types (e.g. dynamic rope)
    def forward(self, x, position_ids):
        inv_freq_expanded = self.inv_freq[None, :, None].float().expand(position_ids.shape[0], -1, 1).to(x.device)
        position_ids_expanded = position_ids[:, None, :].float()

        device_type = x.device.type if isinstance(x.device.type, str) and x.device.type != "mps" else "cpu"
        with maybe_autocast(device_type=device_type, enabled=False):  # Force float32
            freqs = (inv_freq_expanded.float() @ position_ids_expanded.float()).transpose(1, 2)
            emb = torch.cat((freqs, freqs), dim=-1)
            cos = emb.cos() * self.attention_scaling
            sin = emb.sin() * self.attention_scaling

        return cos.to(dtype=x.dtype), sin.to(dtype=x.dtype)

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import importlib.metadata
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil

from .bench import FAMILIES, MAX_NEW_TOKENS, digest, prepare_rows


def sha_file(path):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open('rb') as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(8 << 20), b''):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def write(path, value):
    Path(path).write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2)+'\n')


def verify_weight_stats(manifest):
    """Reuse preparation-time digests for unchanged single-file or sharded weights."""
    expected = manifest.get('weight_stats') or {'model.safetensors':manifest['weight_stat']}
    model = Path(manifest['model_path'])
    for name, recorded in expected.items():
        stat = (model/name).stat()
        if recorded != dict(size=stat.st_size,mtime_ns=stat.st_mtime_ns):
            raise ValueError('model weight file changed since preparation: '+name)


def reviewed_candidate(path, source):
    candidate = json.loads(Path(path).read_text())
    if (candidate['target_revision'],candidate['target_model']) != (source['revision'],source['model_id']):
        raise ValueError('candidate model identity differs')
    if candidate['candidate_id'] != 'MrProBM':
        raise ValueError('this phase contains only the author-selected MrPro-BM')
    target = candidate['target_olmo']
    if not all(target['checks'][key] for key in (
        'finite_positive','strictly_decreasing','fast_band_bitwise_equal',
        'slow_band_bitwise_equal','support_endpoints_bitwise_equal')):
        raise ValueError('BM table fails array constraints')
    queue = dict(max_candidates=10,ordered_candidates=[dict(
        id='MrProBM',eligible=True,review_status='REVIEWED_FOR_GPU',
        definition=candidate['definition'],source_candidate_sha256=sha_file(path),
        hypothesis=candidate['review']['task_hypothesis'],
        failure_rule=candidate['review']['prediction_falsifier'],
        adverse_review=candidate['review']['failure_modes'],
    )],note='Up to ten theoretically defined and reviewed candidates; only BM is ready so far. Earlier psi projection remains rejected. Five minutes is an estimate, not a timeout.')
    tables = {'Native':source['tables']['Native'],'MrPro':source['tables']['MrPro'],
              'MrProBM':{key:target[key] for key in ('values_float32','tensor_sha256','gain')}}
    return tables,queue


def reuse_prepared(args):
    old=args.reuse_prepared.resolve();manifest=json.loads((old/'manifest.json').read_text())
    source=json.loads(args.olmo_source.read_text())
    if args.model.resolve()!=Path(manifest['model_path']):
        raise ValueError('cannot change the model while reusing prepared inputs')
    for name,expected in manifest['prepared_files'].items():
        if sha_file(old/name)!=expected:raise ValueError('prior prepared inputs/configuration drifted')
    weight=(args.model/'model.safetensors').stat()
    if {'size':weight.st_size,'mtime_ns':weight.st_mtime_ns}!=manifest['weight_stat']:
        raise ValueError('model changed since its preparation-time digest check')
    tables,queue=reviewed_candidate(args.candidate,source)
    out=args.out.resolve();shutil.copytree(old,out)
    previous_sha=sha_file(old/'manifest.json')

"""Small mixed RULER screen; task scores use the upstream matching definitions."""
from collections import defaultdict
import re

TASKS = ('niah_single_2', 'niah_multikey_2', 'niah_multiquery', 'vt', 'fwe', 'qa_1')
FAMILIES = dict(zip(TASKS, ('retrieval', 'retrieval', 'retrieval', 'tracking', 'aggregation', 'qa')))


def score(row, text):
    # NVIDIA RULER scripts/eval/evaluate.py postprocessing and synthetic/constants.py
    # matching, expressed on [0, 1] without rounding each individual observation.
    text = re.sub(r'[\x00-\x1f]', '\n', text.strip()).strip().lower()
    hits = [float(reference.lower() in text) for reference in row['references']]
    if not hits:
        raise ValueError('RULER row has no reference answers')
    return max(hits) if row['task'].startswith('qa_') else sum(hits) / len(hits)


def summarize(records):
    cells = defaultdict(list)
    for row in records:
        cells[(row['length_cap'], row['task'])].append(row['correct'])
    by_length = {}
    for length in sorted({key[0] for key in cells}):
        tasks = {task: sum(values) / len(values) for (cap, task), values in cells.items() if cap == length}
        by_length[str(length)] = dict(task_accuracy=tasks, macro_accuracy=sum(tasks.values()) / len(tasks))
    return dict(rows=len(records), score_sum=sum(r['correct'] for r in records),
                by_length=by_length, eos_count=sum(r['ended_eos'] for r in records))


def verdict(candidate, baseline):
    crows, brows = ({r['row_id']: r for r in rows} for rows in (candidate, baseline))
    if len(crows) != len(candidate) or len(brows) != len(baseline) or crows.keys() != brows.keys():
        raise ValueError('comparison has duplicate or unmatched rows')
    if not crows:

# AGENTS.md

1. **Preserve the objective.**
   Do not replace the requested goal with an easier proxy, narrower task, or
   intermediate milestone. Decomposition must advance the original objective.

2. **Act without unnecessary gates.**
   Within the authorized scope, proceed autonomously and resolve routine choices
   using context and judgment. Keep checks proportional to concrete risks and
   applicable requirements; do not invent time limits, approval steps, or workflow
   constraints.

3. **Reason from mechanism and evidence.**
   Ground reasoning in the actual computation, mathematics, implementation, and
   observed results. Challenge key assumptions with counterexamples and competing
   explanations. Distinguish hypotheses and proxy improvements from demonstrated
   outcomes; a complete theory is not a prerequisite for a useful experiment.

4. **Use decision-sufficient experiments.**
   Prefer the least costly experiment that can distinguish competing explanations
   or determine the next decision. Reuse valid baselines and results. Do not reduce
   an experiment below its diagnostic value or increase its scale without a reason.

5. **Use history as evidence.**
   Check relevant prior results, failures, and subsequent corrections before
   repeating a direction. Do not repackage failed assumptions or generalize a
   specific failure beyond its evidence. Successful methods are evidence, not
   immutable constraints.

6. **Finish the real task.**
   Continue through the implementation, verification, analysis, and follow-up the
   task requires. Planning, auditing, partial progress, or reporting are not
   completion unless they are the requested deliverable. Stop when the requested
   outcome is achieved, the user explicitly pauses or changes the objective, or progress
   requires input or resources beyond the authorized scope. State any blocker
   precisely and continue independent work where possible.

Project-specific hypotheses, baselines, parameters, experiment plans, results,
and failure records belong in project documents, not here.

  采用其闭式；实验保持作者另行指定的OLMo 1.485B、4K原生和约5分钟短面板，

## 固定定义及真实数组

对N个中频radix增量，使用

\[
\epsilon_i=\frac{6i(N+1-i)}{N(N+1)(N+2)},\quad i=1,\dots,N,
\qquad m_q=\frac{q(q+1)(3N+2-2q)}{N(N+1)(N+2)}.
\]

频率为\(\omega'_j=\omega_jS^{-m_{j-l}}\)，q在中频段取0..N。
高频及低频槽直接复制实际MrPro数组；gain、base、support端点及标准单表RoPE
operator均与MrPro相同。没有新窗口、长度映射、gain选择或可调曲率。

OLMo使用实际1,484,916,736参数的OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct，revision
`48d788eca847d4d7548f375ad03d3c9312f6139e`；Native4096、base500000、K64、
S4、l14、h32、N18，gain1.138629436111989。只改零基槽15–31。

- [完整候选JSON、两种几何的逐槽值与反向审查](ROPE_MRPRO_BM_CANDIDATE_20260908.json)
- [OLMo全部64槽CSV](ROPE_MRPRO_BM_OLMO_SLOTS_20260908.csv)
- [Qwen源几何与P2逐槽对比CSV，仅供对照](ROPE_MRPRO_BM_QWEN_SLOTS_20260908.csv)
- [生成及独立求解核对程序](../../scripts/analysis/build_boundary_matched_mrpro.py)

OLMo实际FP32频率SHA256：
`fc0f443b1c58601d51209adb7e2b26df7ba10058a9dbdf193eb1de49116d446a`。
全部频率有限、正值、严格递减；实际exponent范围[0,1]，两端频段逐位相同。

## 理论核对与修正

令L为零端点的一维离散Laplacian，粗糙度R=εᵀLε。L正定。
在Σ ε=1下，Lε为常数向量给出唯一极小值；解Lz=1再归一化得到上述闭式。
程序用Fraction精确高斯消元独立求解，未以闭式本身充当优化器验证。
任意非零可行扰动v满足Σv=0时，R(ε+v)−R(ε)=vᵀLv>0。

| OLMo，N18 | MrPro | BM |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| radix粗糙度R | 0.01169590643 | 0.001754385965 |
| 最末中频增量 | 0.1052631579 | 0.01578947368 |
| Σ ε | 1 | 1 |

粗糙度及终端增量均缩至原来的3/(N+2)=15%。**有限槽位下终端增量仍非零，
因此是减小边界跳变，不是完全消除。** R是几何目标，不是任务损失。

严格中频内有
\(m_q^{BM}-m_q^{Mr}=2q(q+1)(N-q)/[N(N+1)(N+2)]>0\)。
每一中频槽都增加压缩；Σ ε相同不表示所有槽的exponent总和相同，后者在OLMo
增加约2.833333。这是必须承认的原生能力代价方向，不能说所有损伤预算均保持。

P2仅在部分中后段也增加压缩；其靠前中频反而比Mr更接近Native。因此，附件中
“与P2方向一致”只可用于部分槽位，不能作为整个BM表的直接效果证据。
新颖性未裁决；最小粗糙度闭式正确不自动证明它是最佳语言模型频率表。

## 前瞻预测、失败原因与分支

本次有界问题是：将过度集中在慢端的间距扩张向中段分配，是否提高这份小面板的
检索、绑定及更新处理综合分数，同时不降低4K分项？

可能失败的明确原因包括：中频普遍加大压缩破坏原生/局部处理；槽间光滑性不能
代表学习到的Q/K敏感性；较早压缩降低所需的距离分辨率；或模型本身无法完成
已冻结任务。不能为这些原因编出无数据支撑的失败概率。

使用[混合RULER面板](ROPE_OLMO_FAST_SCREEN_20260908.md)：6个官方任务，4K两条/16K四条每任务，共36条；随后不同seed、QA错开题号的72条复核。旧自造24条面板与Native compact门槛已被替换，不再用于本轮。真实输入长度、完整官方回答预算、greedy与repetition penalty=1冻结并逐臂复用。

主终点为16K六任务等权均分，4K同口径另报。16K提升且4K不降为开发胜出；16K提升而4K

def load_module(name, path):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--reuse-prepared', required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--upstream', required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--out', required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--seed', type=int, default=SEED)
    parser.add_argument('--short-count', type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument('--long-count', type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument('--long-cap', type=int, default=16384)
    parser.add_argument('--short-cap', type=int, default=4096)

    parser.add_argument('--reuse-nonqa', type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if min(args.short_count, args.long_count) < 1:
        parser.error('sample counts must be positive')
    if args.short_cap <= 0 or args.long_cap <= args.short_cap:
        parser.error('positive short cap and larger long cap required')
    cells = ((args.short_cap, args.short_count), (args.long_cap, args.long_count))

            raise ValueError('reused generator seed/count mismatch')
    old, upstream, out = (p.resolve() for p in (args.reuse_prepared, args.upstream, args.out))
    manifest = json.loads((old/'manifest.json').read_text())
    model_path = Path(manifest['model_path'])
    verify_weight_stats(manifest)
    for name, expected in manifest['model_files_sha256'].items():
        if sha_file(model_path/name) != expected:
            raise ValueError('model metadata changed: ' + name)
    for name in ('tables.json', 'queue.json', 'generation_config.json'):
        if sha_file(old/name) != manifest['prepared_files'][name]:
            raise ValueError('existing preparation changed: ' + name)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)

    metrics = load_module('ruler_metrics', upstream/'scripts/eval/synthetic/constants.py')
    all_rows = []
    source_rows = []
    for cap, count in cells:

            config = definitions[task]
            base = constants.TASKS[config['task']]
            budget = base['tokens_to_generate']
            template = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(

                add_generation_prompt=True) + base.get('answer_prefix', '')
            command = [sys.executable, str(upstream/f"scripts/data/synthetic/{config['task']}.py"),

[exit 0] # 指定ψ投影：完整数组与反向审查

> **后续选择：** 作者另行指定[MrPro-BM闭式](ROPE_MRPRO_BM_PROTOCOL_20260908.md)
> 作为首个准备完成的待测候选。本文件C1原系数、数组及“不推荐GPU”裁决保持不变。

- 日期 / 状态：2026-09-08；指定C1已原样计算并冻结，**不推荐进入GPU队列**。
- 问题：将当前FullLagP2相对MrPro的exponent差异投影到唯一指定的ψ方向，
  得到的表是否符合所述约束，是否有足够的正向理由值得一次真实模型验证？
- 作者最新要求：最多10个候选是上限；每个必须先有明确理论定义、预期作用与反向
  审查，不为凑数量构造曲线或参数网格。本次历史审查只涉及C1；后续候选状态不在此维护。
- 无模型推理、训练或GPU使用；计算程序为
  [project_mrpro_transition.py](../../scripts/analysis/project_mrpro_transition.py)。

## 1. 数组和系数

- [完整JSON：源数组身份、系数、两模型64槽结果及检查](ROPE_MRPRO_TRANSITION_PROJECTION_20260908.json)
- [Qwen逐槽CSV：Native / MrPro / FullLagP2 / C1](ROPE_MRPRO_TRANSITION_QWEN_SLOTS_20260908.csv)
- [OLMo逐槽CSV：Native / MrPro / C1](ROPE_MRPRO_TRANSITION_OLMO_SLOTS_20260908.csv)
- [OLMo既有真实Native/MrPro数组及模型身份](ROPE_OLMO_MRPRO_SOURCE_20260908.json)

系数来自当前实际部署的Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct数组，Native32768、s=4，
MrPro中频边界为零基槽l=23、h=40。明确采用

\[
x_j=\operatorname{clip}((j-l)/(h-l),0,1),\qquad
\psi_j=x_j(1-x_j)(2x_j-1).
\]

“真实exponent”在这里指由实际FP32频率恢复的有效值
\(m_j=-\log(\omega_j^{\rm deployed}/\omega_j^{\rm Native})/\log4\)。
原FullLagP2舍入前audit未在当前checkout及已检查的原服务器阶段目录找到，因此
不把恢复值伪称为舍入前构造数组。源频率tensor SHA已核对；所有64槽恢复值在CSV。

唯一的无截距、无约束、等权最小二乘系数为

\[
a^\star=\frac{\sum_{j=l+1}^{h-1}\psi_j(m_j^{\rm P2}-m_j^{\rm Mr})}
 {\sum_{j=l+1}^{h-1}\psi_j^2}
=\boxed{2.1477690111869907}.
\]

\[
m_j^{\rm C1}=m_j^{\rm Mr}+a^\star\psi_j,\qquad
\omega_j^{\rm C1}=\omega_j^{\rm Mr}4^{-a^\star\psi_j}.
\]

中频内部最后舍入到FP32，其余槽直接复制MrPro值；**没有裁剪exponent、缩小a、
排序或添加第二张修补表**。系数另以输入浮点数的精确Fraction累加独立核对。
唯一性来自非零基向量的严格凸一维二次目标，不是任务能力最优性。

迁移目标为allenai/OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct，revision
`48d788eca847d4d7548f375ad03d3c9312f6139e`，实际1,484,916,736参数，
Native4096、base500000、64个旋转槽。**只迁移上述已固定系数**；使用OLMo自己的
真实MrPro数组和l=14、h=32。没有生成或宣称一个OLMo FullLagP2对照结果。

## 2. 检查结果

| 检查 | Qwen源几何 | OLMo目标几何 |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| 高频段逐位保留 | j≤23，通过 | j≤14，通过 |
| 低频段逐位保留 | j≥40，通过 | j≥32，通过 |
| 全部频率有限、正值、严格递减 | 通过 | 通过 |
| support最小/最大频率端点 | 与MrPro逐位相同 | 与MrPro逐位相同 |
| gain | 1.138629436111989，与MrPro相同 | 同左 |
| exponent范围 | [-0.162752403, 1] | [-0.163779770, 1] |
| 负exponent槽 | 24–29 | 15–20 |
| 最大频率 / Native频率 | 1.253102790 | 1.254888813 |
| 有效改动槽 | 24–39，共16槽 | 15–22、24–31，共16槽 |

base与标准单表RoPE operator不变。OLMo槽23的x=.5使ψ=0，所以该槽也逐位保留。

这里有两个不同的预算事实：两端exponent之差仍为1，因此累计radix乘积仍为4；
另外，对称槽位上的ψ和为0，所以理想实数exponent总和也不变。FP32实现后的
exponent总和误差分别为−1.85e−8和−2.55e−8。它们不能被解释成“能力预算守恒”。

## 3. 反向审查：为什么不推荐当前C1

| 零基槽 | MrPro exponent | FullLagP2 exponent | C1 exponent | 审查 |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- |
| 26 | 0.039216 | 0.008411 | -0.162752 | 越过Native，变成升频 |
| 29 | 0.137255 | 0.221148 | -0.007008 | 与P2方向相反 |
| 30 | 0.183007 | 0.850559 | 0.091203 | 与主要差异方向相反 |
| 31 | 0.235294 | 0.997850 | 0.203819 | 与主要差异方向相反 |
| 32 | 0.294118 | 0.999999 | 0.325593 | 只复现很小一部分移动 |
| 36 | 0.594771 | 1.000000 | 0.799362 | 向P2移动，仍不等于其部署 |
| 39 | 0.888889 | 1.000000 | 0.993807 | 接近P2 |

1. **目标差异主要不是这个零均值方向。** 中频内部P2−Mr的exponent总和为
   +4.844619781，而ψ投影总和为0。这一函数类无法表示前者的正均值分量。
   一维投影只解释差异能量的13.1846%；残差平方和从2.831579713降至2.458247109。
   “误差下降”是最小二乘自带的性质，不能充当选择GPU候选的能力依据。
2. **最关键两槽的作用反了。** ψ在x<.5时为负。第30、31槽恰处于这一侧，
   但P2在这里需要相对Mr增加约.668、.763的exponent。C1分别减少约.092、.031。
   因而不能将其解释为复现当前P2主要转折的简单版本。
3. **保持两端没有保持全部原生频率关系。** 部分中频升到Native之上约25%。
   负exponent是合法旋转，不能据此宣判所有这类方法必败；但本候选的理由没有
   解释这种新外推为什么有利，不能称为单纯“保护原生高频、加强慢频压缩”。
4. **系数跨模型迁移没有获得新的依据。** OLMo原生长度、base和学习到的Q/K
   与Qwen不同。该迁移是作者指定小模型评测下明确记录的设计，不能借用Qwen旧
   分数当作OLMo的有效性证据。

**裁决：** 保留并交付原系数和原数组，不在后台将它修成另一张表；目前不推荐
花GPU验证C1。缺乏正向依据与上述反证足以降低其优先级，不需要先购买一次失败。
不能仅从这些数组给出“失败概率80%”之类数字；也没有证明实际得分必然下降。
本裁决只针对这一固定投影，不能扩展成所有中频分配或所有简单改进均无效。

“长距相位外推代价”是合理的目标方向，但目前给出的只是名称，尚非可直接求解
并宣称理论增益的损失函数。

实际单头logit可以写成\(z=\sum_k z_

# Scientific Brainstorming

## Overview

Scientific brainstorming is a conversational process for generating novel research ideas. Act as a research ideation partner to generate hypotheses, explore interdisciplinary connections, challenge assumptions, and develop methodologies. Apply this skill for creative scientific problem-solving.

## When to Use This Skill

This skill should be used when:
- Generating novel research ideas or directions
- Exploring interdisciplinary connections and analogies
- Challenging assumptions in existing research frameworks
- Developing new methodological approaches
- Identifying research gaps or opportunities
- Overcoming creative blocks in problem-solving
- Brainstorming experimental designs or study plans

## Core Principles

When engaging in scientific brainstorming:

1. **Conversational and Collaborative**: Engage as an equal thought partner, not an instructor. Ask questions, build on ideas together, and maintain a natural dialogue.

2. **Intellectually Curious**: Show genuine interest in the scientist's work. Ask probing questions that demonstrate deep understanding and help uncover new angles.

3. **Creatively Challenging**: Push beyond obvious ideas. Challenge assumptions respectfully, propose unconventional connections, and encourage exploration of "what if" scenarios.

4. **Domain-Aware**: Demonstrate broad scientific knowledge across disciplines to identify cross-pollination opportunities and relevant analogies from other fields.

5. **Structured yet Flexible**: Guide the conversation with purpose, but adapt dynamically based on where the scientist's thinking leads.

## Brainstorming Workflow

### Phase 1: Understanding the Context

Begin by deeply understanding what the scientist is working on. This phase establishes the foundation for productive ideation.

**Approach:**
- Ask open-ended questions about their current research, interests, or challenge
- Understand their field, methodology, and constraints
- Identify what they're trying to achieve and what obstacles they face
- Listen for implicit assumptions or unexplored angles

**Example questions:**
- "What aspect of your research are you most excited about right now?"
- "What problem keeps you up at night?"
- "What assumptions are you making that might be worth questioning?"
- "Are there any unexpected findings that don't fit your current model?"

**Transition:** Once the context is clear, acknowledge understanding and suggest moving into active ideation.

### Phase 2: Divergent Exploration

Help the scientist generate a wide range of ideas without judgment. The goal is quantity and diversity, not immediate feasibility.

**Techniques to employ:**

1. **Cross-Domain Analogies**
   - Draw parallels from other scientific fields
   - "How might concepts from [field X] apply to your problem?"
   - Connect biological systems to social networks, physics to economics, etc.

2. **Assumption Reversal**
   - Identify core assumptions and flip them
   - "What if the opposite were true?"
   - "What if you had unlimited resources/time/data?"

3. **Scale Shifting**
   - Explore the problem at different scales (molecular, cellular, organismal, population, ecosystem)
   - Consider temporal scales (milliseconds to millennia)

4. *

## 发现与推导

原方案符合其整体`|phase|<=pi/2`约束，但这个条件没有保持每一槽原生的
相位范围。Qwen第63槽在原生32K只覆盖`[0,+0.04066305]`；原Carrier在128K
到`-0.36451748`，反向且绝对值为该槽原生上限8.964倍。53..63槽全部反向，
最深槽在32K内也反向快于Native2.241倍。因果attention的相对距离非负，
这些未绕完一圈的慢槽不能借周期性把负角度视为原生见过的正角度。

负频率作为旋转算子完全合法。把权重一起反射可以得到等价参数化，但本次
权重被冻结；共同载波也不是实数logit的规范自由度。上述是精确几何事实，
**并未证明相位区间违反就是全部生成退化的原因**，也不是任何好方法都必须
满足此区间的定理。这里把它选为有既有保守基线支持的设计约束。

保持原B=`{j:omega_j L0<=pi/2}`（47..63）、s=4，并要求所有因果距离
`0<=delta<=s L0`满足：

`0 <= ((omega_j-c)/s)*delta <= omega_j*L0`。

在`c>=0`下，上述条件当且仅当`c<=min_B omega_j`。原背景目标
`A-2Nc+Dc^2`的D为正，因此加入此区间后的唯一极小值为
`clip(N/D,0,min_B omega)`。使用同一份已经冻结的Native均值，不重算背景、不
读取答案优化c，得到`c=min_B omega=1.2409377632138785e-6`。这是解析投影，
不是从多个效果值中选出的裁剪阈值。

新表在B内是`(omega_j-c)/4`；最末槽为0，其他非负且严格递减。组内频差
仍为Native的`/4`。相对同底座最大新增相位在32K为0.010166 rad、128K为
0.040663 rad。所有B槽都落在其自己的原生因果相位区间。这些性质不能替代
任务验证，尤其零频率也可能伤害原有功能。

输出可直接复用。相对原实验2，这同时改变了背景约束与底座，不能把两次实验
之间的变化全部归因于载波裁剪。相对Mr，新候选只有上述低频干预。

> 最终adapter；所有下文“下一项/启动/待执行”均为历史计划，不构成续接指令。
> 结果和复盘以[整夜报告](ROPE_OVERNIGHT_EXPERIMENT_REVIEW_20260908.md)为入口。

- **状态 / 日期：** 2026-09-07 22:33 UTC；数值来源已核对，模型比较尚未执行。
- **替代关系：** 作者要求先解释有效改动，再用小模型提供最少的直接证据。3B
  完整13项队列已在第9项后停止；此前88行Mr比较建议未执行。本协议不延续两者。
- **问题：** 旧Qwen1.5B有效完整p2规则在消除lag采样混叠后，能否用较早结束的
  中频压缩改善远端内容区分，同时保留高频槽原有功能，并在同输入上超过MrPro？

## 数学依据及尚未跨过的缺口

冻结模型的分数是 `Re sum_j C_j exp(i omega_j Delta)`。去掉低频共同载波只将
该段复数响应乘以单位模因子；其频率间隔和复数包络没有增加。正确集合T相对
干扰集合D的注意力log odds变化恰为
`log E[p(.|T)] exp(h) - log E[p(.|D)] exp(h)`，其中h为各key的分数变化。
背景导数能量下降并不决定此量的符号，也不保证最终答案改变。

已有3B短样本缓存只包含提示词末端query。全层重放发现，对所有背景token的
注意力提升可与正确记录相对其他记录下降并存；VT的其他链还属于ICL而非测试
干扰。多数局部输出改变量低于BF16重放误差。这些诊断无法解释实际生成错误，
因此本次保存每次真正生成决策的query；不再据提示词末端head计数选择频率。

此次唯一候选来自已有效的[旧完整p2](ROPE_RECOVERED_QWEN_P2_20260907.json)：
固定Native32768、因果lag权重L-d、条件残差min-max、p=2、SVD rcond=1e-10，
只把2048个取整质心换为全部32768个整数lag。旧质心除末个外均为16b+7；
omega1-omega18近于2pi/8，独立两槽计算重现其旧movement尖峰的99.36%。
完整lag消除此混叠，中段j24..39频率与旧有效表最大相对差仅0.01243%。
QR降维与四槽直接完整最小二乘交叉核对的残差分数最大差3.684e-8。

约8圈处的转折由该固定投影及其数值秩截断产生，不是新设8圈边界。p2及gain
已有历史选择，不能称为全部常数从理论唯一推出；几何冗余也不会自动搬移冻结
模型的内容系数。因此数值修复有确定来源，能力改善仍须直接比较。

## 结果如何改变下一步

- 同gain臂在相同生成前缀的第一处分歧，做状态来源×频率表的层局部交叉重放。
  比较实际输出重放误差，区分表的直接作用与层间状态漂移；低于误差不判断方向。
  此分解是归因诊断，不能代替完整生成得分，也不是能力梯度优化。
- 三项均有直接增益、且同gain控制保留总体增益时，才考虑以固定表做更长长度/
  更大模型的必要确认；不自动扩至完整13项。只赢官方gain而不赢同gain，先归为
  gain或交互效应，不能宣传频率分配胜利。明显退化则停止此候选的长矩阵扩展。
- 首24条平局或分歧太少时，保留不确定性，分析已有具体错误与机制预测；不通过
  新命名、改c、换p或挑任务恢复扫描。不得据本次结果改动已冻结的评分/样本。
- 真实64K LoRA及稀疏编码仍按作者顺序留在后续；本协议不授权提前训练新表。

所有尝试沿用同一GPU锁、唯一job ID和原累计预算；全局截止仍为
**2026-09-08 02:17 UTC**。本协议是一次有来源的候选比较，不重置夜间计数。
原始计划、运行回执和大缓存保留本任务服务器；活动进程只由HANDOFF记录。

> **当前执行入口（2026-09-08）：** 作者已选定唯一[MrPro-BM闭式](ROPE_MRPRO_BM_PROTOCOL_20260908.md)，
> OLMo短评测已完成CPU准备；不继续凑10种方法。原ψ投影按原系数完成并经
> [反向审查](ROPE_MRPRO_TRANSITION_REVIEW_20260908.md)，不推荐GPU。本文件下文保留
> 历史方向及P2分析，具体运行状态以HANDOFF为准。

> **最新方向 / 2026-09-08：** 作者要求从MrRoPE-Pro已有有效方案出发，争取实际
> 分数提升；不再要求先统一解释64K收益、128K检索收益消失与3B任务差异。
> 高频不变、低频`/s`、中频分配及分段边界均为可改的设计选择。不能把作者对
> baseline的概括再变成固定端点约束；也不能把增量改进预先承诺成确定的加分。
> 这覆盖下文“停止所有方法设计、只分析既有证据”的历史范围；具体的bifocal、
> finite-phase replay与13任务确认方案仍撤回，不因方向更新自动复活。

## 0. 当前起点与可改范围

起点改为MrRoPE-Pro完整部署，包括其实际数组、分段、gain、模型和decoder身份。
在它的基础上提出小而明确的频率改动，以任务收益决定是否保留。高、中、低频及
边界均可改，不预先限定只改中频，也不要求同时改遍所有部分。

[原论文§3.2与Appendix B](https://arxiv.org/html/2601.22181v1)把高频保持、低频
总缩放及中频递增radix分配作为方法选择；其中递增指数采用等差序列。论文没有
证明这些选择对任务分数最优。高频保持有保护原位置结构的动机；低频`/s`在目标
最大距离为原范围`s`倍时控制最大相位范围。这些动机都不构成禁止修改的定理，
也不能由放宽它们直接推出能力必升。

已有结果和失败谱系继续用于排除重复的具体干预、支持方向性预测及选择必要比较，
不是新改动必须先解释全部现象的门槛。实验前仍保留与该改动有关的有界问题、
预测和结果分支；不把未验证几何指标冒充任务收益。本次只有方向和来源核对，
未生成新表、实现新方法或运行新模型；运行状态与资源由HANDOFF维护。

## 1. 先把真正需要解释的现象对齐

起点是已有 **FullLagP2 完整部署**。它不是将中段单独移植的 P2Middle。
当前只判断这些差异能否解释并预测已有行为；不从结果形状直接提出另一个候选。

**识别边界：** 现有主要对照同时改变整张表和gain；同gain对照也改变多于31–40的
频率槽，不能直接归因到该段。后续先统一编号：本文表格使用零基索引，差异最大的
零基槽30是第31个旋转对；不能因0/1基混用而漏掉它，也不能假设其它中段完全相同。

[既有配对结果](ROPE_QWEN15_FULL_LAG_P2_RESULT_20260907.json)在
Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct、64K、MK2/VT/FWE各8条上给出：

| 完整部署 | MK2 | VT | FWE |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| FullLagP2 | 37.50 | 87.50 | 70.83 |
| MrRoPE-Pro | 12.50 | 82.50 | 45.83 |
| MrRoPE-Pro，同本方gain | 25.00 | 77.50 | 45.83 |

对官方gain MrPro是9胜13平2负，对同gain参照是10胜12平2负。这里的单位是
24个prompt，不是答案token或attention head。同gain控制说明频率部署在这组数据
确有贡献，但没有定位其作用于哪个距离、层或槽位；不能宣称因果机制已识别。

**128K不是对Mr全面变负。** MK2是0/0，FWE是50/50，VT仍为85/72.5。需要
解决的是两种方法共同的检索地板，以及P2的聚合优势消失。不同长度由生成器
分别构造，不能把它们当成同一题只增加背景的严格长度干预。

**3B应对齐同样的前四条输入。** 用结果owner的逐行分数复算后：

| 任务 | 1.5B64K，同前4条P2−Mr | 3B64K，同4条P2−Mr |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| MK2 | 0.00pp | +25.00pp |
| VT | +10.00pp | −5.00pp |
| FWE | +16.67pp | −8.33pp |

检索迁移没有变差。3B的两个负项各来自一个prompt：VT少答一个变量，FWE少命中
一个词。VT的负行在1.5B也为−1/5，模型变化主要使另一行的+3/5收益消失；
不能把这个旧负行写成3B新出现的定位故障。3B的整体三任务点差仍为正，但很小。
这不是否认退化，而是避免用错误的“整个大模型失败”模式推导新曲线。

[复算JSON](ROPE_P2_TRANSFER_MECHANISM_ANALYSIS_20260908.json)和
[CPU分析代码](../../scripts/analysis/analyze_p2_transfer_mechanism.py)保留对齐方式。
本轮从原结果owner按row ID复算，未重新读取服务器的大prompt/激活。
原始解释和数值修复边界仍归[原协议](ROPE_QWEN15_MINIMAL_MECHANISM_20260907.md)。

## 2. 比较对象必须是完整方法

复用[已有候选文件](ROPE_QWEN15_FULL_LAG_P2_CANDIDATE_20260907.json)，
保留原64个rotary slots及其原顺序：

- 基座：Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，revision
  `989aa7980e4cf806f80c7fef2b1adb7bc71aa306`；无adapter、无优化器更新。
- 本方：原FullLagP2表，gain=`1 + .074 * ln(4)`。
- 对手：原MrPro表，gain=`1 + .1 * ln(4)`。
- 两者均为 **Native32K、静态scale4、目标128K部署**。在物理64K测得的比较，
  是这两个128K部署在64K输入上的效果，不能称为各自按64K重新选择scale2的结果。
- BF16、Flash SDPA；同一模型内使用完整相同prompt与既有decoder、官方逐任务预算
  和评分。1.5B的有效repetition penalty为1.1、EOS151645；本次未从远端重新导出
  3B的完整有效generation config，不把跨checkpoint视为只改变参数量。

MrPro的公式和32/1圈边界及gain按[原论文§3和Appendix B](https://arxiv.org/html/2601.22181v1)
核对。原论文包含Qwen-3B而非本次1.5B；其公布分数不能充当这里的匹配对照。
第一步比较完整部署效果；已有同gain结果只作支持，不将整个增益归因于频率。

[exit 0] 
def verdict(candidate, baseline):
    crows, brows = ({r['row_id']: r for r in rows} for rows in (candidate, baseline))
    if len(crows) != len(candidate) or len(brows) != len(baseline) or crows.keys() != brows.keys():
        raise ValueError('comparison has duplicate or unmatched rows')
    if not crows:
        raise ValueError('empty comparison')
    for key in crows:
        for field in ('prompt_sha256', 'task', 'length_cap', 'references'):
            if crows[key][field] != brows[key][field]:
                raise ValueError('paired row identity differs: ' + key)
    c, b = summarize(candidate), summarize(baseline)
    caps = sorted(c['by_length'], key=int)
    if len(caps) != 2 or int(caps[0]) <= 0 or any(
            set(cell['task_accuracy']) != set(TASKS) for cell in c['by_length'].values()):
        raise ValueError('incomplete task/length panel')
    deltas = {cap: c['by_length'][cap]['macro_accuracy'] - b['by_length'][cap]['macro_accuracy']
              for cap in c['by_length']}
    status = ('DEVELOPMENT_WIN' if deltas[caps[-1]] > 0 and deltas[caps[0]] >= 0 else
              'TRADEOFF' if deltas[caps[-1]] > 0 else 'NO_LONG_GAIN')
    return dict(status=status, macro_delta_by_length=deltas, candidate=c, baseline=b,
                paired_wins=sum(crows[k]['correct'] > brows[k]['correct'] for k in crows),
                paired_losses=sum(crows[k]['correct'] < brows[k]['correct'] for k in crows),
                baseline_extreme_cells=[f'{cap}/{task}' for cap, cell in b['by_length'].items()
                    for task, value in cell['task_accuracy'].items() if value in (0, 1)],
                evidence_scope='Six-task RULER development subset; not full RULER or independent confirmation')

from scripts.lib.rope.gap_capped import cumulative_minimum
from scripts.analysis.build_boundary_matched_mrpro import roughness,increments

def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(8 << 20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def canonical_sha256(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(

[exit 0] #!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Download and freeze LongBench subsets into local jsonl files.

This makes downstream eval deterministic and offline-friendly.
Supports fallback data source selection for environments without HuggingFace access:
- `hf`: HuggingFace datasets
- `modelscope`/`dashscope`: Alibaba ecosystem mirror (via ModelScope SDK)
- `auto`: try HF first, then fallback to ModelScope
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from datasets import load_dataset


def parse_csv(text: str) -> List[str]:
    return [x.strip() for x in text.split(",") if x.strip()]


def now() -> str:
    return time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")


def longbench_cfg_candidates(task: str) -> List[str]:
    base = task.strip()
    candidates: List[str] = []
    for cfg in (base, base.lower(), f"{base}_e", f"{base.lower()}_e"):
        if cfg not in candidates:
            candidates.append(cfg)
    return candidates


def _load_hf(task: str, split: str) -> Tuple[object, str, str]:
    errors: List[str] = []
    for cfg in longbench_cfg_candidates(task):
        try:
            ds = load_dataset("THUDM/LongBench", cfg, split=split, trust_remote_code=True)
            return ds, cfg, "hf:THUDM/LongBench"
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append(f"cfg={cfg}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    raise RuntimeError(f"[hf] Cannot load LongBench task={task}\n" + "\n".join(errors))


def _extract_split_obj(raw_obj: object, split: str) -> Optional[object]:
    if raw_obj is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(raw_obj, dict):
        if split in raw_obj:
            return raw_obj[split]
        if "test" in raw_obj:
            return raw_obj["test"]
        if "validation" in raw_obj:
            return raw_obj["validation"]
    if hasattr(raw_obj, split):
        return getattr(raw_obj, split)
    return raw_obj


def _as_indexable_dataset(raw_obj: object, split: str) -> object:
    obj = _extract_split_obj(raw_obj, split)
    if obj is None:
        raise RuntimeError("Dataset object is empty after split extraction.")

    # HF Dataset and most wrappers satisfy this.
    if hasattr(obj, "__len__") and hasattr(obj, "__getitem__"):
        return obj

    # ModelScope dataset wrappers often provide conversion helpers.
    for method in ("to_hf_dataset", "to_dataset"):
        if hasattr(obj, method):
            converted = getattr(obj, method)()
            converted = _extract_split_obj(converted, split)
            if converted is not None and hasattr(converted, "__len__") and hasattr(converted, "__getitem__"):
                return converted
    raise RuntimeError(f"Unsupported dataset object type: {type(obj)}")


def _load_modelscope(task: str, split: str) -> Tuple[object, str, str]:
    try:
        from modelscope.msdatasets import MsDataset
    except Exception as e:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "[modelscope] Missing dependency. Install with: pip install modelscope\n"
            f"Import error: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
        ) from e

    errors: List[str] = []
    dataset_name_candidates = [
        "THUDM/LongBench",
        "AI-ModelScope/LongBench",
        "LongBench",
    ]
    for cfg in longbench_cfg_candidates(task):
        for dname in dataset_name_candidates:
            for kw in (
                {"dataset_name": dname, "subset_name": cfg, "split": split},
                {"dataset_name": dname, "subset_name": cfg},
            ):
                try:
                    raw = MsDataset.load(**kw)
                    ds = _as_indexable_dataset(raw, split=split)
                    return ds, cfg, f"modelscope:{dname}"
                except Exception as e:
                    errors.append(f"dataset={dname}, cfg={cfg}, kw={kw}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"

def choose_bucket(total_tokens: int, native_context_length: int) -> BucketDecision:
    total = int(total_tokens)
    native = int(native_context_length)
    if total < 0 or native <= 0:
        raise ValueError("token counts and native context length must be positive")
    if total <= native:
        return BucketDecision("retention", total, 0, native)
    if total <= 2 * native:
        return BucketDecision("near", total, native, 2 * native)
    if total <= 4 * native:
        return BucketDecision("far", total, 2 * native, 4 * native)
    return BucketDecision(
        None,
        total,
        4 * native,
        4 * native,
        reason="prompt plus generation reserve exceeds 4 * L_native",
    )


def _token_ids(value: Any) -> list[int]:
    if hasattr(value, "tolist"):
        value = value.tolist()
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        value = value["input_ids"]
    if value and isinstance(value[0], list):
        value = value[0]
    return [int(item) for item in value]


def render_chat_prompt(tokenizer: Any, content: str) -> tuple[list[int], str]:
    if not hasattr(tokenizer, "apply_chat_template"):
        raise TypeError("target tokenizer must expose apply_chat_template")
    messages = [{"role": "user", "content": content}]
    tokenized = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
        messages,
        add_generation_prompt=True,
        tokenize=True,
    )
    ids = _token_ids(tokenized)
    try:
        rendered = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
            messages,
            add_generation_prompt=True,
            tokenize=False,
        )
    except TypeError:
        rendered = content
    return ids, str(rendered)

    temporary.replace(path)

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BucketDecision:
    name: str | None
    total_tokens: int
    lower_exclusive: int
    upper_inclusive: int
    reason: str | None = None


def sha256_bytes(value: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()


def sha256_text(value: str) -> str:
    return sha256_bytes(value.encode("utf-8"))


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(8 << 20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def canonical_json(value: Any) -> bytes:
    return json.dumps(
        value,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    ).encode("utf-8")


def row_sha256(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return sha256_bytes(canonical_json(row))


def tokenizer_tree_sha256(root: Path) -> str:
    """Hash tokenizer/config files without reading or loading model weights."""

    files = []
    for path in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
        if not path.is_file() or path.name.endswith(".safetensors"):
            continue
        relative = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        files.append(
            {
                "path": relative,
                "bytes": int(path.stat().st_size),
                "sha256": sha256_file(path),
            }
        )
    if not files:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"no tokenizer/config files under {root}")
    return sha256_bytes(canonical_json(files))


def load_json(path: Path) -> Any:
        if value is None or int(value) <= 0:
            raise ValueError("official GovReport generation budget is missing")
        return int(value), "LongBench dataset2maxlen.json"
    return int(qa_reserve), "target-free QA reserve contract"


def load_longbench_prompt_assets(longbench_root: Path) -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, int]]:
    config = longbench_root / "official_config"
    prompts = load_json(config / "dataset2prompt.json")
    budgets = load_json(config / "dataset2maxlen.json")
    if not isinstance(prompts, dict) or not isinstance(budgets, dict):
        raise ValueError("LongBench official config files must contain objects")
    return {str(key): str(value) for key, value in prompts.items()}, {
        str(key): int(value) for key, value in budgets.items()
    }


def build_longbench_row(
    row: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    task: str,
    family: str,
    prompt_template: str,
    tokenizer: Any,
    tokenizer_sha256: str,
    native_context_length: int,
    generation_reserve: int,
    generation_reserve_source: str,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any] | None, str | None]:
    context = row.get("context")
    question = row.get("input")
    if not isinstance(context, str) or not isinstance(question, str):
        return None, "row lacks string context or input"
    references = row.get("answers")
    if not isinstance(references, list) or not references:
        return None, "row lacks nonempty answers"
    try:
        content = prompt_template.format(context=context, input=question)
    except KeyError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"official prompt has unsupported field: {exc}") from exc
    input_ids, rendered = render_chat_prompt(tokenizer, content)
    total = len(input_ids) + int(generation_reserve)
    decision = choose_bucket(total, native_context_length)
    if decision.name is None:
        return None, decision.reason
    digest = row_sha256(row)
    record = {
        "source_family": family,
        "task": task,
        "source_id": str(row.get("_id", digest)),
        "row_sha256": digest,
        "source_context_sha256": sha256_text(context),
        "prompt_sha256": sha256_text(content),
        "rendered_chat_prompt_sha256": sha256_text(rendered),
        "tokenizer_sha256": tokenizer_sha256,
        "input_ids": input

"""Compare one completed candidate-only run against an archived matched reference."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

from scripts.experiments.olmo_fast_screen.prepare import sha_file
from scripts.experiments.olmo_fast_screen.ruler_bench import score, summarize, verdict


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--run', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--method', required=True)
    p.add_argument('--baseline', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--out', type=Path, required=True)
    args = p.parse_args()
    run = args.run
    status = json.loads((run/'status.json').read_text())
    receipt = json.loads((run/(args.method+'.json')).read_text())
    if status['status'] != 'COMPLETE' or receipt['status'] != 'COMPLETE':
        raise ValueError('candidate run is incomplete')
    raw = run/(args.method+'.jsonl')
    if sha_file(raw) != receipt['raw_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('candidate raw checksum differs')
    runtime = json.loads((run/'runtime.json').read_text())
    candidate = list(map(json.loads, raw.read_text().splitlines()))
    baseline_all = list(map(json.loads, args.baseline.read_text().splitlines()))
    reference = {r['row_id']: r for r in baseline_all}
    if len(reference) != len(baseline_all):
        raise ValueError('duplicate archived baseline rows')
    if [r['row_id'] for r in candidate] != receipt['row_ids'] or receipt['row_ids'] != runtime['row_ids']:
        raise ValueError('candidate row order differs from frozen runtime')
    baseline = [reference[r['row_id']] for r in candidate]
    eos = runtime['generation_config']['eos_token_id']
    eos = {eos} if isinstance(eos, int) else set(eos)
    for c, b in zip(candidate, baseline):
        for field in ('prompt_sha256', 'references', 'task', 'length_cap', 'input_tokens'):
            if c[field] != b[field]:
                raise ValueError('candidate and reference identity differs: '+field)
        if abs(score(c, c['output_text'])-c['correct']) > 1e-12:
            raise ValueError('candidate score differs on recomputation')
        if abs(score(b, b['output_text'])-b['correct']) > 1e-12:
            raise ValueError('baseline score differs on recomputation')
        if c['ended_eos'] != (c['generated_ids'][-1] in eos):
            raise ValueError('candidate termination mismatch')
        if len(c['generated_ids']) > c['max_new_tokens']:
            raise ValueError('candidate exceeded frozen budget')
    result = verdict(candidate, baseline)
    details = []
    for c, b in zip(candidate, baseline):
        details.append(dict(row_id=c['row_id'], prompt_sha256=c['prompt_sha256'],
            candidate_score=c['correct'], baseline_score=b['correct'],
            delta=c['correct']-b['correct'], candidate_output=c['output_text'],
            baseline_output=b['output_text'], candidate_ids=c['generated_ids'],
            baseline_ids=b['generated_ids'], candidate_eos=c['ended_eos'], baseline_eos=b['ended_eos']))
    report = dict(model_id=runtime['model_id'], revision=runtime['revision'],
        method=args.method, result=result, run_status=status,
        baseline_reused=True, files_sha256=dict(candidate=sha_file(raw), baseline=sha_file(args.baseline),
            runtime=sha_file(run/'runtime.json')), rows=details,
        scope='Matched six-task development subset, not full RULER. Official recall and complete-string/EOS are distinct.')
    args.out.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps(dict(status=result['status'], delta=result['
...[clipped 173 chars]

这里按原官方评分判定，完整字符串、token IDs和EOS另存，未更换判分标准。

## 该负结果改变什么

闭式和独立LP验证正确，候选确实满足更小原生频率位移及原最大gap限制，但
两项都不足以选出更好的部署。它还压缩了过渡区、增加了大gap的数量及粗糙度，
改变了长距联合相位关系；不能将这些几何事实任一项单独指定为失败的唯一原因。

这个结果不支持继续扫描cap、挪动边界或把表和BM插值以修当前分数。也不能据此
声称所有原生保护方法无效。用户进一步明确不要进入“改善一个代理、破坏另一项、
十分钟换一个猜想”的循环；下一项CausalGain仅有CPU代码，暂停未运行。
先建立对原生关系与长距区分具有联合判别力的分析，不能把又一个proxy最优解
自动送到GPU。

及完成回执SHA通过；本地解包的16个实际执行源码hash与runtime一致。

`results/bm_transfer_20260908/code_gap_capped_01/`；远端同名目录位于
`/root/autodl-tmp/bm_transfer_20260908/`。GPU进程已结束。

# 10小时零训练研究：从诊断走向实际收益

用户2026-09-08授权约10小时自主负责实验，从14:15 UTC开始，使用既有4080
SUPER 32GB服务器。用户后续明确约10小时不是硬截止：未解决就继续直到用户
睡醒回来；只有跨模型、全量任务的可比验证支持彻底完成，才授权关闭服务器。
README核心目标是从MrRoPE-Pro出发，开发相同模型、输入和评分下有真实长任务
收益的零训练位置编码方法。BM不是目标的定义，也不要求先证明普遍最优理论。

## 已有证据决定第一步

OLMo S4 BM在独立六任务小面板有有效局部收益；Qwen3B固定BM在128K未胜MrPro。
刚完成的两例交叉缓存诊断显示，成败随前缀形成表而非最后query/生成期的全局
读取表变化。这不识别具体层组，更不能推出BM是所有模型的错误方向。

历史9/2 learned headwise clocks及9/3 Selective-31已经读过：它们涉及OLMo、不同
频率方向、学习或敏感性选择、不同数据，既不支持直接重跑旧选择器，也不否定
本次Qwen的层间互补假设。不按phase能量、KL/PPL或光滑性单独选择能力方案。

[exit 0] """Download a pinned public checkpoint through a mirror, checking official SHAs."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

os.environ['HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET'] = '1'
os.environ['HF_HUB_DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT'] = '30'
from huggingface_hub import hf_hub_download


def main():
    root = Path('/root/autodl-tmp/bm_transfer_qwen7b_20260908')
    metadata = Path('/root/autodl-tmp/qwen7b_metadata')
    spec = json.loads((metadata/'asset_source.json').read_text())
    model = root/'model'
    model.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if shutil.disk_usage(root).free < sum(x['size'] for x in spec['weights'].values())+2_000_000_000:
        raise RuntimeError('insufficient free space; no cleanup performed')
    shutil.copyfile(metadata/'asset_source.json', root/'asset_source.json')
    for name, expected in spec['metadata_sha256'].items():
        data = (metadata/name).read_bytes()
        if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != expected:
            raise ValueError('official metadata transfer mismatch')
        (model/name).write_bytes(data)

    def download(name):
        path = Path(hf_hub_download(repo_id=spec['model_id'], filename=name,
            revision=spec['revision'], local_dir=model, endpoint='https://hf-mirror.com',
            token=False, etag_timeout=15))
        sha = hashlib.sha256()
        with path.open('rb') as stream:
            for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(8 << 20), b''):
                sha.update(chunk)
        if sha.hexdigest() != spec['weights'][name]['sha256'] or path.stat().st_size != spec['weights'][name]['size']:
            raise ValueError('mirror weights differ from pinned official identity')
        print(json.dumps(dict(file=name, status='OFFICIAL_SHA_MATCHED')), flush=True)

    (root/'download_status.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='DOWNLOADING', pid=os.getpid())))
    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
            list(pool.map(download, spec['weights']))
    except Exception as exc:
        (root/'download_status.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='FAILED', error_type=type(exc).__name__)))
        raise
    (root/'download_status.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(status='COMPLETE', revision=spec['revision'])))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

[exit 0] """Scalar or slotwise rotary amplitudes for explicitly declared experiments."""
import numpy as np
import torch

from scripts.experiments.cross_audit.tables import install_static, verify_static, tensor_sha


def gain_vector(table, device):
    values = np.asarray(table['gain_by_slot'], dtype=np.float32)
    if values.shape != (len(table['values_float32']),) or not np.isfinite(values).all() or np.any(values <= 0):
        raise ValueError('invalid slotwise rotary amplitudes')
    # OLMo uses rotate_half: each rotary pair occupies coordinates j and j+K.
    return torch.from_numpy(np.concatenate((values, values))).to(device)


def install(model, table):
    values = np.asarray(table['values_float32'], dtype=np.float32)
    rotary = install_static(model, values, table['gain'])
    if 'gain_by_slot' in table:
        rotary.attention_scaling = gain_vector(table, rotary.inv_freq.device)


def verify(model, table):
    values = np.asarray(table['values_float32'], dtype=np.float32)
    if 'gain_by_slot' not in table:
        return verify_static(model, values, table['gain'])
    rotary = model.model.rotary_emb
    if rotary.inv_freq.dtype != torch.float32 or tensor_sha(rotary.inv_freq.detach().cpu().numpy()) != table['tensor_sha256']:
        raise RuntimeError('runtime frequency drift')
    expected = gain_vector(table, rotary.inv_freq.device)
    if not torch.equal(rotary.attention_scaling, expected):
        raise RuntimeError('runtime slotwise amplitude drift')

def load_module(name, path):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module

状态：GPU已启动，方案与选择在查看自然任务结果前确定。不是新频率候选。

任务”的规则，保留同一模型、BM数组、gain与S4，检验自然任务范围。Qwen3B
失败不抹除OLMo局部收益，也不使BM成为已解决的通用方法。

## 输入与比较

- OLMo-2-0425-1B-Instruct，实际1,484,916,736参数，原模型revision及完整元数据
  复用`prepared_bm_02`，不下载或更改权重。
- HotpotQA、2WikiMQA、Qasper；从既有来源SHA排序的未截断LongBench token池，
  各取前60条。原池分别171/199/197条，原官方源各200条。
- 已核对原始zip中的context与参考答案、现tokenizer解码与旧完整chat prompt SHA，
  原文和问题均完整保留。没有补答案、拼接背景、padding或截断原文。
- 输出预算分别32/32/128，输入+预算必须<=16384；输入真实长度单独报告。
  原token池按旧64-token reserve已筛过长例，不声称这是原官方200条的全量。
- 这三个自然QA面板未找到同表/输入的MrPro基线；现有E1_MrPro_long是构造
  double_evidence任务，不能冒充匹配自然基线。因此只补跑一次MrPro并归档，再
  跑固定BM；后续相同输入复用此次Mr结果。

主读数：输入>4096的自然QA token-F1，按任务等权；<=4096另报。保留完整生成
和EOS，不把F1当完整字符串正确率，不声称证据位于固定16K距离。本池曾用于
旧项目工作，不叫全新盲确认；BM未按此池答案选择或修改。

def atomic(path, value):
    path = Path(path)
    temporary = path.with_suffix(path.suffix+'.tmp')
    temporary.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2)+'\n')
    temporary.replace(path)


def rows(path):
    return [json.loads(line) for line in Path(path).read_text().splitlines() if line]


def eligible_queue(queue):
    entries = queue['ordered_candidates']
    limit=queue.get('max_candidates',10)
    if not isinstance(limit,int) or not 1<=limit<=10:
        raise ValueError('invalid candidate limit')
    if len(entries) > limit or len({e['id'] for e in entries}) != len(entries):
        raise ValueError('candidate queue exceeds limit or has duplicate ids')
    selected = []
    for item in entries:
        if item['eligible']:
            if item['review_status'] != 'REVIEWED_FOR_GPU' or not all(item.get(k) for k in
                ('definition','hypothesis','failure_rule')):
                raise ValueError('candidate lacks a completed theoretical review')
            selected.append(item['id'])
    return selected


def worker(prepared, out, phase_deadline=None, baseline_only=False):
    prepared, out = Path(prepared), Path(out)
    manifest = json.loads((prepared/'manifest.json').read_text())
    for name, expected in manifest['prepared_files'].items():
        if sha_file(prepared/name) != expected:
            raise ValueError('prepared file drift: '+name)
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
    for name, expected in manifest['code_files'].items():
        if sha_file(root/name) != expected:
            raise ValueError('code drift: '+name)
    queue = eligible_queue(json.loads((prepared/'queue.json').read_text()))
    if not queue and not baseline_only:
        raise ValueError('no theoretically reviewed candidate; GPU load refused')
    if baseline_only:
        queue = []
    model_path = Path(manifest['model_path'])
    verify_weight_stats(manifest)
    for name, expected in manifest['model_files_sha256'].items():
        if sha_file(model_path/name) != expected:
            raise ValueError('model metadata/tokenizer drift: '+name)
    for name, expected in manifest['software'].items():
        if importlib.metadata.version(name) != expected:
            raise ValueError('prep

[exit 0] """CPU-only selection of disjoint long calibration documents and natural QA."""
import argparse,hashlib,json,zipfile
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pyarrow.parquet as pq
from transformers import AutoTokenizer


def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--model',required=True);p.add_argument('--parquet',required=True)
    p.add_argument('--longbench',required=True);p.add_argument('--out',required=True);a=p.parse_args()
    root=Path(a.out);root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False);tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(a.model,local_files_only=True)
    docs=[];row=0
    for batch in pq.ParquetFile(a.parquet).iter_batches(batch_size=128,columns=['text']):
        for text in batch.column(0).to_pylist():
            row+=1
            if len(text)<100000:continue
            ids=tok.encode(text,add_special_tokens=False)
            if len(ids)<32769:continue
            sha=hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()
            if any(d['sha256']==sha for d in docs):continue
            name=f'doc_{len(docs):02d}.npy';np.save(root/name,np.asarray(ids[:32769],dtype=np.int32))
            docs.append(dict(file=name,sha256=sha,source_row=row-1,source=a.parquet,split='C' if len(docs)<8 else 'V',tokens=32769))
            print(json.dumps({'selected_calibration_docs':len(docs),'source_row':row}),flush=True)
            if len(docs)==16:break
        if len(docs)==16:break
    if len(docs)!=16:raise RuntimeError(f'need 16 long real documents, found {len(docs)}')
    rows=[];counts={'native':0,'long':0}
    template='You are given a story, which can be either a novel or a movie script, and a question. Answer the question as concisely as you can, using a single phrase if possible. Do not provide any explanation.\n\nStory: {context}\n\nNow, answer the question based on the story as concisely as you can, using a single phrase if possible. Do not provide any explanation.\n\nQuestion: {input}\n\nAnswer:'
    with zipfile.ZipFile(a.longbench) as z:
        for i,line in enumerate(z.open('data/narrativeqa.jsonl')):
            r=json.loads(line);content=template.format(**r)
            ids=tok.apply_chat_template([{'role':'user','content':content}],tokenize=True,add_generation_prompt=True,return_dict=False)
            if not isinstance(ids,list) or not all(isinstance(t,int) for t in ids):raise TypeError('expected flat token ID list')
            bucket='native' if 4096<=len(ids)<=16384 else 'long' if 32768<len(ids)<=65408 else None
            if bucket is None or counts[bucket]>=8:continue
            sha=hashlib.sha256(r['context'].encode()).hexdigest()
            if sha in {d['sha256'] for d in docs}:raise RuntimeError('calibration/task overlap')
            record={'row_id':f'narrativeqa_{i}','task':'narrativeqa','bucket':bucket,'ids':ids,'references':r['answers'],'budget':128,'source_index':i,'context_sha256':sha,'input_tokens':len(ids)}
            rows.append(record);counts[bucket]+=1
            if min(counts.values())==8:break
    if min(counts.values())<8:raise RuntimeError(f'QA buckets insufficient: {counts}')
    with (root/'eval.jsonl').open('w') as f:
        for r in rows:f.write(json.dumps(r)+'\n')
    files={f.name:hashlib.sha256(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for f in root.iterdir() if f.is_file()}
    (root/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps({'status':'COMPLETE','docs':docs,'eval_counts':counts,'files':files,'scope':'pilot; first eligible rows, natural task subset, not full LongBench'},indent=2))
if __name__=='__main__':main()

"""Paired tail-token NLL on frozen natural documents; no generation claim."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import time
import numpy as np
from .prepare import sha_file, verify_weight_stats, write


def prepare(a):
    import pyarrow.parquet as pq
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    manifest = json.loads((a.prepared/'manifest.json').read_text())
    source = json.loads(a.source_manifest.read_text())
    chosen = source['docs']
    if len({d['source'] for d in chosen}) != 1:
        raise ValueError('expected one source parquet')
    by_row = {d['source_row']: d for d in chosen}
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(manifest['model_path'], local_files_only=True)
    a.out.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    docs = []
    offset = 0
    for batch in pq.ParquetFile(chosen[0]['source']).iter_batches(batch_size=256, columns=['text']):
        for i, text in enumerate(batch.column(0).to_pylist()):
            if offset+i not in by_row:
                continue
            old = by_row[offset+i]
            if hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest() != old['sha256']:
                raise ValueError('source content changed')
            ids = tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)
            if len(ids) < max(a.lengths)+1:
                raise ValueError('selected document too short for frozen lengths')
            name = old['file']
            np.save(a.out/name, np.asarray(ids[:max(a.lengths)+1], dtype=np.int32))
            docs.append(dict(file=name, sha256=sha_file(a.out/name), source_row=offset+i,
                             text_sha256=old['sha256'], original_tokens=len(ids)))
        offset += len(batch)
        if len(docs) == len(chosen):
            break
    if len(docs) != len(chosen):
        raise ValueError('source rows missing')
    write(a.out/'manifest.json', dict(docs=docs, lengths=a.lengths, tail_tokens=512,
        model_manifest_sha256=sha_file(a.prepared/'manifest.json'),
        source_manifest_sha256=sha_file(a.source_manifest),
        scope='Existing 16 selected long FineWeb-Edu documents retokenized for target. Tail 512 next-token NLL, not full-document NLL or generation capability.'))

            raise ValueError('model metadata changed')
    if sha_file(a.prepared/'tables.json') != manifest['prepared_files']['tables.json']:
        raise ValueError('tables changed')

$ /bin/zsh -lc cat scripts/experiments/olmo_fast_screen/ruler_bench.py
[exit 0] """Small mixed RULER screen; task scores use the upstream matching definitions."""
from collections import defaultdict
import re

TASKS = ('niah_single_2', 'niah_multikey_2', 'niah_multiquery', 'vt', 'fwe', 'qa_1')
FAMILIES = dict(zip(TASKS, ('retrieval', 'retrieval', 'retrieval', 'tracking', 'aggregation', 'qa')))


def score(row, text):
    # NVIDIA RULER scripts/eval/evaluate.py postprocessing and synthetic/constants.py
    # matching, expressed on [0, 1] without rounding each individual observation.
    text = re.sub(r'[\x00-\x1f]', '\n', text.strip()).strip().lower()
    hits = [float(reference.lower() in text) for reference in row['references']]
    if not hits:
        raise ValueError('RULER row has no reference answers')
    return max(hits) if row['task'].startswith('qa_') else sum(hits) / len(hits)


def summarize(records):
    cells = defaultdict(list)
    for row in records:
        cells[(row['length_cap'], row['task'])].append(row['correct'])
    by_length = {}
    for length in sorted({key[0] for key in cells}):
        tasks = {task: sum(values) / len(values) for (cap, task), values in cells.items() if cap == length}
        by_length[str(length)] = dict(task_accuracy=tasks, macro_accuracy=sum(tasks.values()) / len(tasks))
    return dict(rows=len(records), score_sum=sum(r['correct'] for r in records),
                by_length=by_length, eos_count=sum(r['ended_eos'] for r in records))


def verdict(candidate, baseline):
    crows, brows = ({r['row_id']: r for r in rows} for rows in (candidate, baseline))
    if len(crows) != len(candidate) or len(brows) != len(baseline) or crows.keys() != brows.keys():
        raise ValueError('comparison has duplicate or unmatched rows')
    if not crows:
        raise ValueError('empty comparison')
    for key in crows:
        for field in ('prompt_sha256', 'task', 'length_cap', 'references'):
            if crows[key][field] != brows[key][field]:
                raise ValueError('paired row identity differs: ' + key)
    c, b = summarize(candidate), summarize(baseline)
    caps = sorted(c['by_length'], key=int)
    if len(caps) != 2 or int(caps[0]) <= 0 or any(
            set(cell['task_accuracy']) != set(TASKS) for cell in c['by_length'].values()):
        raise ValueError('incomplete task/length panel')
    deltas = {cap: c['by_length'][cap]['macro_accuracy'] - b['by_length'][cap]['macro_accuracy']
              for cap in c['by_length']}
    status = ('DEVELOPMENT_WIN' if deltas[caps[-1]] > 0 and deltas[caps[0]] >= 0 else
              'TRADEOFF' if deltas[caps[-1]] > 0 else 'NO_LONG_GAIN')
    return dict(status=status, macro_delta_by_length=deltas, candidate=c, baseline=b,
                paired_wins=sum(crows[k]['correct'] > brows[k]['correct'] for k in crows),
                paired_losses=sum(crows[k]['correct'] < brows[k]['correct'] for k in crows),
                baseline_extreme_cells=[f'{cap}/{task}' for cap, cell in b['by_length'].items()
                    for task, value in cell['task_accuracy'].items() if value in (0, 1)],
                evidence_scope='Six-task RULER development subset; not full RULER or independent confirmation')

[exit 0] """Reuse source-verified, untruncated LongBench prompts for OLMo BM task transfer."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import zipfile

from transformers import AutoTokenizer

from .bench import digest
from .prepare import sha_file, verify_weight_stats, write

TASKS = ('hotpotqa', '2wikimqa', 'qasper')
BUDGETS = {'hotpotqa': 32, '2wikimqa': 32, 'qasper': 128}


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--parent', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--data-root', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--out', type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument('--limit-per-task', type=int, default=0)
    p.add_argument('--offset-per-task', type=int, default=0)
    args = p.parse_args()
    parent = args.parent
    manifest = json.loads((parent/'manifest.json').read_text())
    verify_weight_stats(manifest)
    tokenized = args.data_root/'tokenized/olmo2_target_free'
    source_manifest = json.loads((tokenized/'token_manifest.json').read_text())
    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(manifest['model_path'], local_files_only=True)
    rows = []
    sources = {}
    with zipfile.ZipFile(args.data_root/'longbench/data.zip') as archive:
        for task in TASKS:
            cell = source_manifest['longbench']['cells']['longbench:'+task]
            path = tokenized/cell['rows_path']
            if sha_file(path) != cell['rows_sha256']:
                raise ValueError('frozen source tokens changed')
            raw = list(map(json.loads, archive.read('data/'+task+'.jsonl').decode().splitlines()))
            candidates = list(map(json.loads, path.read_text().splitlines()))
            selected = []
            for old in candidates:
                ids = old['input_ids']; original = raw[old['source_row_index']]
                if old['input_tokens'] != len(ids):
                    raise ValueError('token count differs')
                if hashlib.sha256(original['context'].encode()).hexdigest() != old['source_context_sha256']:
                    raise ValueError('source document differs')
                if old['references'] != original['answers']:
                    raise ValueError('answers differ from original source')
                rendered = tokenizer.decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=False)
                if (hashlib.sha256(rendered.encode()).hexdigest() != old['rendered_chat_prompt_sha256']
                        or original['context'] not in rendered or original['input'] not in rendered):
                    raise ValueError('chat/tokenizer roundtrip or complete source differs')
                if len(ids)+BUDGETS[task] > 16384:
                    continue
                selected.append(dict(row_id=task+'_'+str(old['source_row_index']),
                    source_id=old['source_id'], source_row_index=old['source_row_index'],
                    task=task, family='natural_qa', length_cap=16384,
                    native_stratum='native' if len(ids) <= 4096 else 'extended',
                    prompt_ids=ids, prompt_sha256=digest(ids), input_tokens=len(ids),
                    max_new_tokens=BUDGETS[task], references=old['references']))
            # Stable pre-existing source-hash order, no outcome-based selection.
            selected = selected[args.offset_per_task:]
            if args.limit_per_task:
                selected = selected[:args.limit_per_task]
            rows.extend(selected)
            sources[task] = dict(source_tokens_sha256=sha_file(path), original_rows=len(raw),
                frozen_available_rows=len(candidates), selected_rows=len(selected))
    args.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    for name in ('tables.json', 'generation_config.json'):
        if sha_file(parent/name) != manifest['prepared_files'][name]:
            raise ValueError('parent table/decoder changed')
        shuti
